*sa ya evam etad rājanaṁ devatāsu protaṁ vedaitāsām eva devatānāṁ+ sa-lokatāṁ+ sārñṭitāṁ+ sāyujyaṁ gacchati sarvam āyur eti jyog jīvati mahān prajayā paśubhir bhavati mahān kīrtyā brāhmaṇān na nindet tad vratam.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows the rājana sāma intertwined with the devatās attains the worlds of the devatās with equal wealth and enjoyment. He has good health, is free of disease, has many children and animals and has great fame. He should not criticize devatās. That is the vow.**

**Комментарий:**

Ete vai devāḥ prayakñaṁ yad brāhmaṇāḥ: the brāhmaṇas are directly the devatās.

Thus brāhmaṇa means devatās (devatāsu).

### 21

### 2.21.1

*trayī vidyā hiṁ-kāras traya ime lokāḥ sa prastāvo 'gnir vāyur ādityaḥ sa udgītho nakñatrāṇi vayāṁ+si marīcayaḥ sa pratihāraḥ sarpā gandharvāḥ pitaras tan nidhanam etat sāma sarvasmin protam.*

**Перевод:**

**The three Vedas are hiṁkāra. The three worlds are prastāva. Fire, air and the sun are the udgītha. The constellations, birds and rays are the pratihāra. Snakes, Gandharvas and the Pitṛs are the nidhana. This the sāma in general intertwined with everything.**

### 2.21.2

*sa ya evam etat sāma sarvasmin protaṁ veda sarvaṁ+ ha bhavati.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows the sāman intertwined with everything attains all desires.**

### 2.21.3

*tad eña ślokaḥ. yāni pañcadhā trīṇi trīṇi tebhyo na jyāyaḥ param anyad asti.*

**Перевод:**

**There is a verse concerning this. There is nothing superior to five part of the sāman with groups of threes.**

### 2.21.4

*yas tad veda sa veda sarvaṁ+ sarvā diśo balim asmai haranti sarvam asmīty upāsīta tad vrataṁ tad vratam.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this knows everything. All beings bring offerings to him from all directions.**

**He should meditate “I am everything.” That is the vow. That is the vow.**

**Комментарий:**

Since the sāman is contemplated as everything, he should meditate on nondifference with that sāman.

### 22

### 2.22.1

*vinardi sāmno vṛṇe paśavyam ity agner udgītho 'niruktaḥ prajāpater niruktaḥ somasya mṛdu ślakñṇaṁ vāyo bala-vad indrasya krauñcaṁ bṛhaspater apadhvāntaṁ varuṇasya tān sarvān evopaseveta vāruṇaṁ tv eva varjayet.*

**Перевод:**

**One should accept the vinardi singing related to the Sāma Veda, for benefit of animals. The udgītha for the fire devatā is sung in an unclear manner. This is pleasing to Agni. The song with clear notes is pleasing to Prajāpati. The verses for the moon are gentle and the verses for Vāyu are gentle. The song for Indra is chanted with great effort. The song for Bṛhaspati sounds like the cry of an osprey. The song for Varuṇa sounds like a broken gong. One should chant them all except Varuṇa’s song.**

**Комментарий:**

Having shown the different types of worship of sāmans, different types of songs of the udgāta are explained. Vinardi means verses with special types of chanting of svaras.

### 2.22.2

*amṛtatvaṁ devebhya āgāyānīty āgāyet svadhāṁ pitṛbhya āśāṁ manuñyebhyas tṛṇodakaṁ paśubhyaḥ svargaṁ lokaṁ yajamānāyānnam ātmana āgāyānīty etāni manasā dhyāyann apramattaḥ stuvati.*

**Перевод:**

**One should sing to attain immortality for the devatās by that singing, oblations for the Pitṛs, aspiration for humans, grass and water for animals, heaven for the sponsor of the sacrifice, and food for oneself. Thinking in this way with the mind, one praises giving attention to the proper svāras.**

### 2.22.3

*sarve svarā indrasyātmānaḥ sarva ūñmāṇaḥ prajāpater ātmānaḥ sarve sparśā mṛtyor ātmānas taṁ yadi svareñūpālabhetendraṁ+ śaraṇaṁ prapanno 'bhūvaṁ sa tvā prati vakñyatīty enaṁ brūyāt.*

**Перевод:**

**All the vowels are like the limbs of Indra. All the silibants are like the limbs of Prajāpati. All the consonants are like the limbs of death. If someone criticizes the singer for the pronunciation of vowels, one should say to him “I have surrendered to Indra. He will speak to you.”**

### 2.22.4

*atha yady enam ūñmasūpālabheta prajāpatiṁ+ śaraṇaṁ prapanno 'bhūvaṁ sa tvā prati pekñyatīty enaṁ brūyād atha yady enaṁ+ sparśeñūpālabheta mṛtyuṁ+ śaraṇaṁ prapanno 'bhūvaṁ sa tvā prati dhakñyatīty enaṁ brūyāt.*

**Перевод:**

**If someone criticizes the pronunciation of the silibants, one should say “I have surrendered to Prajāpati. He will crush you into powder.” If someone criticizes the singer for his pronunciation of the consonants, one should say, “I have surrendered to death. He will burn you up.”**

### 2.22.5

*sarve svarā ghoña-vanto bala-vanto vaktavyā indre balaṁ dadānīti sarva ūñmāṇo 'grastā anirastā vivṛtā vaktavyāḥ prajāpater ātmānaṁ paridadānīti sarve sparśā leśenānabhinihitā vaktavyā mṛtyor ātmānaṁ pariharāṇīti.*

**Перевод:**

**All the vowels should be pronounced loudly with strength, while thinking “May I give strength to Indra.” All the silibants should be pronounced without inhaling, without exhaling and fully audible while thinking “May I gain for myself from Prajāpati.” All the consonants should be pronounced without mixing while thinking, “May I be free of faults from death.”**

### 23

### 2.23.1

*trayo dharma-skandhā yajño 'dhyayanaṁ dānam iti. prathamas tapa eva dvitīyo brahma-cāryācarya-kula-vāsī tṛtīyo 'tyantam ātmānam ācārya-kule 'vasādayan sarva ete puṇya-lokā bhavanti brahma-saṁstho 'mṛtatvam eti.*

**Перевод:**

**There are three divisions of dharma. The first division is yajñā, study of the Vedas and charity. The second is austerity. The third is living in the hermitage of the guru and practicing brahmacarya, exhausting the body there. All these lead to pious planets. The person fixed in Brahman attains immortality.**

**Комментарий:**

In order to explain worship of Brahman in the form of oṁkāra, three types of dharma are praised. Sacrifice, study of the Vedas and charity indicate the gṛhastha. Austerity indicates vānaprastha and sannyāsa āśramas, since they have a prominence of austerity. Austerity means inflicting suffering on the body. Exhausting the body (ātmānam) by following rules at the house of the guru, while practicing brahmacarya is the third. Among the four āśramas following the three types of dharma, he who is fixed in Brahman attains liberation. Those who simply follow the āśrama rules attain pious planets. Being fixed in Brahman does not refer only to the sannyāsa āśrama.

### 2.23.2

*prajāpatir lokān abhyatapat tebhyo 'bhitaptebhyas trayī vidyā samprāsravat tām abhyatapat tasyā abhitaptāyā etāny akñarāṇi samprāsravanta bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Prajāpati meditated on the three worlds. From meditating on the three worlds the three Vedas emerged. He meditated on the three Vedas. From meditating on the three Vedas the syllables bhur, bhuvaḥ and svaḥ arose.**

**Комментарий:**

Omkāra is praised in order to speak about it as worship of Brahman. Prajāpati meditated on the worlds to extract their essence. Meditating on the worlds, the three Vedas emerged as their essence.

### 2.23.3

*tāny abhyatapat tebhyo 'bhitaptebhya oṁ-kāraḥ samprāsravat tad yathā śaṅkunā sarvāṇi parṇāni santṛṇṇāny evam oṁ-kāreṇa sarvā vāk santṛṇṇoṁ-kāra evedaṁ+ sarvam oṁ-kāra evedaṁ+ sarvam.*

**Перевод:**

**He meditated on the three syllables. From meditating on the syllables omkāra arose. As all leaves are spread with veins, oṁkāra spreads in all words. Oṁkāra thus constitutes all words.**

**Комментарий:**

Thus by oṁkāra one should worship Brahman.

### 24

### 2.24.1

*brahma-vādino vadanti yad vasūnāṁ prātaḥ-savanaṁ+ rudrāṇāṁ mādhyan-dinaṁ+ savanam ādityānāṁ ca viśveñāṁ ca devānāṁ tṛtīya-savanam.*

**Перевод:**

**The knowers of Brahman say that the morning oblation belongs to the Vasus, the noon oblation belongs to the Rudras and the third oblation belongs to the Viśvadeva and Ādityas.**

**Комментарий:**

Related to worship, some aṅgas of karma are praised. Vasus are in in charge of earth, Rudras are in charge of the antarikña and the sun and Viśvadevas are in charge of the heavens.

### 2.24.2

*kva tarhi yajamānasya loka iti sa yas taṁ na vidyāt kathaṁ kuryād atha vidvān kuryāt.*

**Перевод:**

**Since these lokas are controlled by the devatās etc. what planet remains for the sponsor of the sacrifice? If he does not know, how can he perform the sacrifice? He should know.**

**Комментарий:**

Knowing the characteristics of uttering the mantras for the homas of the sāma chanting, one should perform his actions.

### 2.24.3

*purā prātar anuvākasyopākaraṇāj jaghanena gārhapatyasyodaṅ-mukha upaviśya sa vāsavaṁ sāmābhigāyati.*

**Перевод:**

**Before reciting the anuvāka in the morning, the priest sits facing north behind the gārhapatya fire and chants the vāsava sāma.**

### 2.24.4

*loka-dvāram apāvārṇū paśyema tvā vayaṁ+rā huṁ ā jyā yo ā iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Open the door to the earth planet. Let us see you for attaining a kingdom.**

**Комментарий:**

O Agni! Open the door for attaining the earth planet. May we see you by the open door for attaining a kingdom!

### 2.24.5

*atha juhoti namo 'gnaye pṛthivīkñate loka-kñite lokaṁ me yajamānāya vindaiña vai yajamānasya loka etāsmi.*

**Перевод:**

**Then he offers oblations, saying “I offer respects to Agni, who lives on earth, who rules earth. Obtain this planet for me, the sacrificer. This is the planet of the sacrificer.”**

**Комментарий:**

After this he offers oblations using the following mantra. The homa mantra is this.

### 2.24.6

*atra yajamānaḥ parastād āyuñaḥ svāhāpajahi parigham ity uktvottiñthati tasmai vasavaḥ prātaḥ-savanaṁ samprayacchanti.*

**Перевод:**

**“After death, I, the sacrificer, go to that planet. Remove the bolt from the door to the planet. Svāhā.” Saying that, he stands and offers oblations. The Vasus give him the earth, related to the morning oblation.**

**Комментарий:**

After death (ayuñaḥ parastāt) I, the sacrifice (yajamānasya), go (etā asmi) to that planet. Remove the bolt from the door to the planet. Svāhā. Saying that mantra, he stands. The Vasus give him the earth, related to the morning oblation (prāthaḥ savanam).

### 2.24.7

*purā mādhyan-dinasya savanasyopākaraṇāj jaghanenāgnidhrīyasyodaṅ-mukha upaviśya sa raudraṁ sāmābhigāyati.*

**Перевод:**

**Before the noon oblations, the sacrificer sits behind the agnidhriya fire facing north and chants the rudra sāman.**

### 2.24.8

*lo3ka-dvāram apāvārṇū paśyema tvā vayaṁ vairā huṁ ā jyā yo ā iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Open the door for attaining antarikña. May we see you by the open door for attaining the planet of antarikña.**

### 2.24.9

*atha juhoti namo vāyave 'ntarikña-kñite loka-kñite lokaṁ me yajamānāya vindaiña vai yajamānasya loka evāsmi.*

**Перевод:**

**Then he offers oblations, saying “I offer respects to Vāyu, who lives in the antarikña, who rules antarikña. Obtain this planet for me, the sacrificer. This is the planet of the sacrificer.**

### 2.24.10

*atra yajamānaḥ parastād āyuñaḥ svāhāpajahi parigham ity uktvottiñthati tasmai rudrā mādhyan-dinaṁ+ savanaṁ+ samprayacchanti.*

**Перевод:**

**After death, I, the sacrificer, go to that planet. Remove the bolt from the door to the planet. Svāhā.” Saying that, he stands. The rudras give him the antarikña, related to the noon oblation.**

### 2.24.11

*purā tṛtīya-savanasyopākaraṇāj jaghanenāhavanīyasyodaṅ-mukha upaviśya sa ādityaṁ+ sa vaiśva-devaṁ sāmabhigāyati.*

**Перевод:**

**Before the third oblations, the sacrificer sits behind the ahavanīya fire facing north and chants the vaiśvadeva and āditya sāman.**

### 2.24.12

*lo3ka-dvāram apāvā3rṇū33 paśyema tvā vayaṁ svārā33333 huṁ3 ā33 jyā3 yo3 ā32111 iti*  
*ādityam*

**Перевод:**

**“Open the door for attaining Svarga. May we see you by the open door for attaining the planet of Svarga. This is for the sun.”**

### 2.24.13

*atha vaiśva-devaṁ loka-dvāram apāvārṇū paśyema tvā vayaṁ sāmrā huṁ ā jyā yo ā iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Then for the vaiśvadevas.**

**Open the door for attaining Svarga. May we see you by the open door for attaining the planet of excellent Svarga.**

### 2.24.14

*atha juhoti namo āditebhyaś ca viśvebhyaś ca devebhyo divi-kñidbhyo loka-kñidbhyo lokaṁ me yajamānāya vindata.*

**Перевод:**

**Then he offers oblations, saying “I offer respects to the ādityas and viśvadevas, who live in the heavens, who rule the heavens. Obtain this planet for me, the sacrificer. This is the planet of the sacrificer.”**

### 2.24.15

*eña vai yajamānasya loka etāsmy atra yajamānaḥ parastād āyuñaḥ svāhāpahata parigham ity uktvotiñṭhati.*

**Перевод:**

**“This is the planet of the sacrificer. After death, I, the sacrificer, go to that planet. Remove the bolt from the door to the planet. Svāhā.” Saying that, he stands.**

### 2.24.16

*tasmā ādityāś ca viśve ca devās tṛtīyaṁ+ savanaṁ+ samprayacchanty eña ha vai yajñasya mātrāṁ veda ya evaṁ veda ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The ādītyas and viśvadevas give him the heavenly planets, related to the third oblation. He who knows this knows sacrifice as it is.**

## Part 3

## 1 - Meditation on the sun as honey

### 3.1.1

*oṁ asau vā ādityo deva-madhu tasya dyaur eva tiraśīna-vaṁ śo 'ntarikñam apūpo marīcayaḥ putrāḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**This āditya (sun) is honey for the devatās. The heaven of this honey is the cross beam. The antarikña is the honeycomb. The vapors caused by the sun are the bee larva.**

**Комментарий:**

The sun is a cause of pleasure for the devatās. Thus he is called honey. One should think of the sun as honey. The heaven is the cross bean as it is the support. The support of the honey (sun) is the heaven, from which hangs down the honeycomb or antarikña from the heavens. Marīci refers to the water from earth drawn up by the sun and situated in the sun’s rays. They are like bee larva in the hive.

### 3.1.2

*tasya ye prāñco raśmayas tā evāsya prāñcyo madhu-nāḍyaḥ. ṛca eva madhu-kṛta ṛg-veda eva puñpaṁ tā amṛtā āpas*

**Перевод:**

**The eastern rays are the eastern honey cells. The Rg mantras are the bees. The rituals of the Rg Veda are the flowers. The oblations are the nectar.**

**Комментарий:**

Apaḥ refers to the oblations, consisting of soma, ghee and milk, offered into the fire.

### 3.1.3

*tā vā etā ṛcaḥ etam ṛg-vedam abhyatapaṁs tasyābhitaptasya yaśas teja indriyaṁ vīryam annādyaṁ raso 'jāyata.*

**Перевод:**

**The mantras stimulate the Ṛg veda rituals. From the aroused rituals arose fruit in the form of fame, power, senses, strength and abundant food.**

**Комментарий:**

The verses stimulate the Vedic rituals, like bee (verses) taking nectar from flowers (Vedic rituals). Karmas of the Ṛg veda undertaken using Ṛg mantras extract the essence of soma ghee etc,. forming honey. This is like flowers sucked by bees and producing nectar. The result is fame, power, senses, strength and food.

### 3.1.4

*tad vyakñarat tad ādityam abhito 'śrayat tadvā etad yad etad ādityasya rohitaṁ+ rūpam.*

**Перевод:**

**That result flowed and took shelter of the sun. Thus the sun has a red form.**

**Комментарий:**

The results become visible as fame etc. One should meditate on the sun as fame, etc.

### 2

### 3.2.1

*atha ye' sya dakñiṇā raśmayas tā evāsya dakñiṇā madhu-nāḍyo yajūṁ+ñy eva madhu-kṛto yajur-veda eva puñpaṁ tā amṛtā āpaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The southern rays are the southern honey cells. The Yajur mantras are the bees. The rituals of the Yajur Veda are the flower. The oblations are the nectar.**

### 3.2.2

*tāni vā etāni yajūṁñy etaṁ yajur-vedam abhyatapaṁ+s tasyābhitaptasya yaśas teja indriyaṁ vīryam annādyaṁ+ raso 'jāyata.*

**Перевод:**

**The mantras arouse the Yajur Veda rituals. From the aroused rituals arose nectar in the form of fame, power, senses, strength and abundant food.**

### 3.2.3

*tad vyakñarat tad ādityam abhito 'śrayat tadā etad yad etad ādityasya śuklaṁ+ rūpam.*

**Перевод:**

**That result flowed and took shelter of the sun. Thus the sun has a white form.**

### 3

### 3.3.1

*atha ye 'sya pratyañco raśmayas tā evāsya pratīcyo madhu-nāḍyaḥ sāmāny eva madhu-kṛtaḥ sāma-veda eva puñpaṁ tā amṛtā āpaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The wetern rays are the western honey cells. The SāmaVeda mantras are the bees. The rituals of the Sāman Veda are the flower. The oblations are the nectar.**

### 3.3.2

*tāni vā etāny sāmāny etaṁ+ sāma-vedam abhyapataṁ+s tasyābhitaptasya yaśas teja indriyaṁ vīryam annādyaṁ+ raso 'jāyata.*

**Перевод:**

**The mantras aroused up the Sāma Veda rituals. From the aroused rituals arose nectar in the form of fame, power, senses, strength and abundant food.**

### 3.3.3

*tad vyakñarat tad ādityam abhito 'śrayat tadā etad yad etad ādityasya paraṁ kṛñṇaṁ+ rūpam.*

**Перевод:**

**That result flowed and took shelter of the sun. Thus the sun as a black form.**

### 4

### 3.4.1

*atha ye 'syodañco raśmayas tā evāsyodīcyo madhu-nāḍyo 'tharvāṅgirasa eva madhu-kṛta itihāsa-purāṇaṁ puñpaṁ tā amṛtā āpaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The northern rays are the northern honey cells. The Atharva mantras are the bees. The itihāsas and purāṇas karmas are the flower. The oblations are the nectar.**

### 3.4.2

*te vā ete tharvāṅgirasa etad itihāsa-purāṇam abhyapataṁ+s tasyābhitaptasya yaśas teja indriyaṁ vīryam annādyaṁ+ raso 'jāyata.*

**Перевод:**

**The mantras heated up the itihāsa and purāṇa rituals. From the heated rituals arose nectar in the form of fame, power, senses, strength and abundant food.**

### 3.4.3

*tad vyakñarat tad ādityam abhito 'śrayat tadā etad yad etad ādityasya paraṁ kṛñṇaṁ+ rūpam.*

**Перевод:**

**That result flowed and took shelter of the sun. Thus the sun as an intense black form.**

### 5

### 3.5.1

*atha ye 'syordhvā raśmayas tā evāsyordhvā madhu-nāḍyo guhyā evādeśā madhu-kṛto brahmaiva puñpaṁ tā amṛtā āpaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The upward rays are the northern honey cells. The secret doctrines about Brahman are the bees. Brahman is the flower. The oblations are the nectar.**

### 3.5.2

*te vā ete guhyā ādeśā etad brahmābhyapataṁ+s tasyābhitaptasya yaśas teja indriyaṁ vīryam annādyaṁ+ raso 'jāyata.*

**Перевод:**

**The secret teachings aroused Brahman. From the aroused Brahman arose nectar in the form of fame, power, senses, strength and abundant food.**

### 3.5.3

*tad vyakñarat tad ādityam abhito 'śrayat tadā etad yad etad ādityasya madhye kñobhata iva.*

**Перевод:**

**That result flowed and took shelter of the sun. That seems to move about in the center of the sun.**

**Комментарий:**

People of steady vision gazing upon the sun see this moving form.

### 3.5.4

*te vā ete rasāṇāṁ+ rasā vedā hi rasās teñām ete rasās tāni vā etāny amṛtānām amṛtāni vedā hy amṛtās teñām etāny amṛtāni.*

**Перевод:**

**The colors are the essence of the essences. The Vedas are the essence. These are the essence of the Vedas. These are the nectar of all nectar. Vedas are nectar. These are the nectar of the Vedas.**

**Комментарий:**

These colors are the essence since they arise from the karmas prescribed in the Vedas, which are the essence of the worlds. They are the nectar since they give enjoyment produced by the karmas of the Vedas.

The sun’s rays of various directions are the honey cells. The karmas of the Vedas are the flowers. The mantras, like bees. take the oblations of soma, ghee and milk to the sun planet. This produces fame, power, senses, strength and food. Five types of nectar—red, white, black, very black and moving become situated in the sun.

### 6

### 3.6.1

*tad yat prathamam amṛtaṁ tad vasava upajīvanty agninā mukhena na vai devā aśnanti na pibanty etad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Vasus, live on the first nectar (red) through the mouth of Agni. The devatās do not eat or drink, but seeing the nectar, they are satisfied.**

**Комментарий:**

The five nectars are enjoyed by the Vasus, Rudras, Ādityas, Maruts and Sādhyas. Those who worship them become Vasus etc. and then attain Brahman. Attaining the red form of fame, etc. by all their senses, the Vasus are satisfied.

### 3.6.2

*ta etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśanty etasmād rūpād udyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Vasus enter that red form and then rise from that form.**

**Комментарий:**

Experiencing that red form, after enjoyment they become indifferent. They then become delighted.

### 3.6.3

*sa ya etad evam amṛtaṁ veda vasūnām evaiko bhūtvāgninaiva mukhenaitad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyati sa eva etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśaty etasmād rūpād udeti.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this nectar, becoming one of the Vasus, seeing the nectar through the mouth of Agni, becomes satisfied. Entering that nectar he rises from that nectar.**

### 3.6.4

*sa yāvad ādityaḥ purastād udetā paścād astam etā vasūnām eva tāvad ādhipatyaṁ+ svārājyaṁ paryetā.*

**Перевод:**

**As long as the sun rises in the east and sets in the west, he enjoys lordship and sovereignty among the Vasus.**

**Комментарий:**

Svārājyam means his fulfilment of desires remains unobstructed.

### 7

### 3.7.1

*atha yad dvitīyam amṛtaṁ tad rudrā upajīvantīndreṇa mukhena na vai devā aśnanti na pibanty etad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Rudras, live on the second nectar (white) through the mouth of Indra. The devatās do not eat or drink, but seeing the nectar, they are satisfied.**

### 3.7.2

*ta etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśanty etasmād rūpād udyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Rudras enter that form of nectar and rise from it.**

### 3.7.3

*sa ya etad evam amṛtaṁ veda rudrāṇām evaiko bhūtvendreṇaiva mukhenaitad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyati sa etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśaty etasmād rūpād udeti.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this nectar, becoming one of the Rudras, seeing the nectar through the mouth of Indra, becomes satisfied. Entering that nectar he rises from that nectar.**

### 3.7.4

*sa yāvad ādityaḥ purastād udetā paścād astametā dvis tāvad dakñiṇata udetottarato 'stam etā rudrāṇām eva tāvad ādhipatyaṁ+ svārājyaṁ paryetā.*

**Перевод:**

**As long as the sun rises in the east and sets in the west, it takes twice as long to rise in the south and set in the north. For that time he enjoys lordship and sovereignty among the Rudras.**

### 8

### 3.8.1

*atha yat tṛtīyam amṛtaṁ tad ādityā upajīvanti varuṇena mukhena na vai devā aśnanti na pibanty etad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The ādtiyas live on the third nectar (black) through the mouth of Varuṇa. The devatās do not eat or drink, but seeing the nectar, they are satisfied.**

### 3.8.2

*ta etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśanty etasmād rūpād udyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The ādityas enter that form of nectar and rise from it.**

### 3.8.3

*sa ya etad evam amṛtaṁ vedādityānām evaiko bhūtva varuṇena mukhenaitad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyati sa etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśaty etasmād rūpād udeti.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this nectar, becoming one of the Ādityas, seeing the nectar through the mouth of Varuṇa, becomes satisfied. Entering that nectar, he rises from that nectar.**

### 3.8.4

*sa yāvad ādityo dakñiṇata udetottarato 'stametā dvis tāvad paścād udetā purastād astametādityānām eva tāvad ādhipatyaṁ+ svārājyaṁ paryetā.*

**Перевод:**

**As long as the sun rise in the south and set in the north, twice the time it takes to rise in the west and set in the east. For that time, he enjoys lordship and sovereignty among the ādityas.**

### 9

### 3.9.1

*atha yat caturtham amṛtaṁ tad maruta upajīvanti somena mukhena na vai devā aśnanti na pibanty etad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Maruts live on the fourth nectar (very black) through the mouth of Soma. The devatās do not eat or drink, but seeing the nectar, they are satisfied.**

### 3.9.2

*ta etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśanty etasmād rūpād udyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Maruts enter that form of nectar and rise from it.**

### 3.9.3

*sa ya etad evam amṛtaṁ ved a marutām evaiko bhūtva somena mukhenaitad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyati sa etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśaty etasmād rūpād udeti.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this nectar, becoming one of the Maruts, seeing the nectar through the mouth of Soma, becomes satisfied. Entering that nectar, he rises from that nectar.**

### 3.9.4

*sa yāvad ādityaḥ paścād udetā purastād astametā dvis tāvad uttarata udetā dakñiṇato 'stametā marutām eva tāvad ādhipatyaṁ+ svārājyaṁ paryetā.*

**Перевод:**

**As long as the sun rises in the west and sets in the east, it takes twice as long to rise in the north and set in the south. For that time he enjoys lordship and sovereignty among the Maruts.**

### 10

### 3.10.1

*atha yat pañcamam amṛtaṁ tat sādhyā upajīvanti brahmaṇā mukhena na vai devā aśnanti na pibanty etad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Sādhyas live on the fifth nectar (very black) through the mouth of Brahmā. The devatās do not eat or drink, but seeing the nectar, they are satisfied.**

### 3.10.2

*ta etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśanty etasmād rūpād udyanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The Sādhyas enter that form of nectar and rise from it.**

### 3.10.3

*sa ya etad evam amṛtaṁ veda sādhyānām evaiko bhūtva brahmaṇaiva mukhenaitad evāmṛtaṁ dṛñṭvā tṛpyati sa etad eva rūpam abhisaṁviśaty etasmād rūpād udeti.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this nectar, becoming one of the Sādhyas, seeing the nectar through the mouth of Brahmā, becomes satisfied. Entering that nectar, he rises from that nectar.**

### 3.10.4

*sa yāvad ādityo uttarata udetā dakñiṇato 'stametā dvis tāvad ūrdhva udetā 'rvāṅ astametā sādhyānām eva tāvad ādhipatyaṁ+ svārājyaṁ paryetā.*

**Перевод:**

**As long as the sun rises in the north and sets in the south, it takes twice as long to rise upwards and set downwards. For that time he enjoys lordship and sovereignty among the Sādhyas.**

### 11

### 3.11.1

*atha tata ūrdhvaṁ udetya naivodetā nāñtam etaikala eva madhye sthātā tad eña ślokaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**Having risen upwards, he does not rise or set. Alone he remains in the middle. On this there is a verse.**

**Комментарий:**

The worship of kārya brahman named Āditya, which has various colored nectars within it enjoyed by the Vasus, Rudras, Ādityas, Maruts and Sādhyas, and which has rising and setting, was taught. Now the meditation of Brahman who has the jīva (called Aditya) as his body, and who is devoid of kārya Brahman and devoid of (material) name, form and activity, is taught.

### 3.11.2

*na vai tatra sa nimloca nodiyāya kadācana*  
*devās tenāhaṁ+ satyena mā virādhiñi brahmaṇeti*

**Перевод:**

**O devas! In that state of freedom from Aditya, the antaryāmī of the sun has is no setting or rising at any time. May I not act contrary to the unchanging Brahman.**

### 3.11.3

*na ha vā asmā udeti na nimolocati sakṛd divā haivāsmai bhavati ya etām evaṁ brahmopaniñadaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**For he who understands this knowledge of honey, the sun does not rise or set. There is continuous day for him.**

**Комментарий:**

He realizes all objects continuously.

### 3.11.4

*tad dhaitad brahmā prajāpataya uvāca prajāpatir manave manuḥ prajābhyas tad dhaitad uddālakāruṇaye jyeñṭhāya putrāya pitā brahma provāca.*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā spoke this knowledge to Prajāpati. Prajāpati spoke it to Manu. Manu spoke it to his descendants. The father spoke this knowledge of Brahman to Uddālakāruni, his eldest son.**

### 3.11.5

*idaṁ vāva taj jyeñṭhāya putrāya pitā brahma prabrūyāt praṇāyyāya vānte-vāsine.*

**Перевод:**

**A father should instruct this knowledge of Brahman to his eldest son, or to a qualified disciple.**

### 3.11.6

*nānyasmai kasmaicana yady apy asmā imām adbhiḥ parigṛhītāṁ dhanasya pūrṇāṁ dadyād etad eva tato bhūya ity etad eva tato bhūya iti.*

**Перевод:**

**One should not teach this knowledge to anyone else, even if one were to give that student the earth full of wealth, surrounded by the ocean. This knowledge is greater than that. It is greater than that.**

## 12 - Gāyatrī-vidyā

### 3.12.1

*gāyatrī vā idaṁ sarvaṁ bhūtaṁ yad idaṁ kiñca vāg vai gāyatrī vāg vā idaṁsarvaṁ bhūtaṁ gāyati ca trāyate ca.*

**Перевод:**

**Gāyatrī is all beings that exist. Speech is gāyatrī. Speak is all beings. Speech sings and delivers.**

**Комментарий:**

This section explains how Brahman is similar to gāyatrī, having four sections each with six syllables. For instance indraḥ śacipatiḥ, balena pīḍitaḥ, duscyavano vṛñā, samutsu sāsahi is divided into four parts (catuñpada), each with six syllables called ñaḍvidha.

The four parts of Brahman in this section are all beings, the earth, the body and the heart. Brahman as all beings has singing and delivering as its qualities. Brahman as earth has the qualities of sustaining all beings and limiting them to the earth. Brahman as the body and as the heart have the qualities of supporting prāṇa and limiting the prāṇa. Thus as gāyatrī has four parts and six types, Brahman has four parts and six types.

This does not refer to the famous gāyatrī mantra, since that gāyatrī is not all beings. Gāyatrī here means Brahman or the Lord. Vai is for emphasis. Brahman alone is composed of all beings. Speech is gāyatrī means that Brahman, which is similar to gāyatrī has the form of speech. One should meditate on the similarity of Brahman and gāyatrī.

Brahman with that form of speech or śabda is all beings, all jīvas. It delivers through prescribing what is beneficial and forbidding what is dangerous. Brahman is called gāyatrī because it sings about all beings and delivers all beings. Consisting of all beings, Brahman has two functions: praising all beings through words and delivering all beings through words.

### 3.12.2

*yā vai sā gāyatrīyaṁ vāva sā yeyaṁ prṭhivy āsyāṁ+ hīdaṁ+ sarvaṁ bhūtaṁ pratiñṭhitam etām eva nātiśīyate.*

**Перевод:**

**That Brahman or gāyatrī which is all beings is earth. All beings are supported on the earth. They do not surpass the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

This presents the second part of Brahman. That Brahman is the earth. How is earth Brahman? It supports all beings. That power is given by Brahman. Earth by itself does not have that power. The beings born on earth (etām), subject to karma, cannot surpass the earth (atiśiyate). All beings remain within the universe made of earth. This second aspect of Brahman (earth) has two qualities: it supports all beings and prevents the beings from going beyond the earth.

### 3.12.3

*yā vai sā pṛthivīyaṁ vāva sā yad idam asmin puruñe śarīram asmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiñṭhitā etad eva nātiśīyante.*

**Перевод:**

**That earth is the body of the jīva. The prāṇas are established in the body. They do not go beyond the body.**

**Комментарий:**

The third aspect of Brahman is explained. Puruñe means in the jīva endowed with a body. Brahman, called gāyatrī, having earth as its second aspect, is the body of the jīva (puruñe). The body, composed of Brahman, supports the prāṇas and limited them to the body. Brahman, or gāyatrī, which is all beings and the earth, is the third pāda called “body.”

### 3.12.4

*yad vai tat puruñe śarīram idaṁ vāva yad tad idam asminn antaḥ puruñe hṛdayam asmin hīme prāṇāḥ pratiñṭhitā etad eva nātiśīyante.*

**Перевод:**

**That body of the jīva is the heart of the jīva. In the heart, the prāṇas are established. The prāṇas do not go beyond it.**

**Комментарий:**

The fourth aspect of Brahman is explained. Brahman in the form of gāyatrī, called the body, is the heart of the jīva, which is also a form of Brahman. Prāṇāḥ refers to the five prāṇas or to the senses. They are established in the heart by nāḍīs. Like the third aspect, the heart supports the prāṇas and restricts them to the heart.

### 3.12.5

*saiñā catuñ-padā ñaḍ-vidhā gāyatrī tad etad ṛcābhyanūktam.*

**Перевод:**

**The gāyatrī has four parts and six functions. This is described in the Ṛg mantra.**

### 3.12.6

*tāvān asya mahimā*  
*tato jyāyāṁ+ś ca pūruñaḥ*  
*pādo 'sya sarvā bhūtāni*  
*tri-pād asyāmṛtaṁ divīti*

**Перевод:**

**The greatness of what was described is limited to this. The Lord surpasses the greatness of what was described. All beings are but one part. The immortal three parts belonging to the Lord are in the spiritual world.**

**Комментарий:**

The doubt may arise that if Brahman has these four divisions and six functions it becomes divided into parts.

All jīvas in contact with the material world are one pāda. They are only one portion of the Lord’s greatness. Divi means in the non-material place beyond material world. Asya means the Lord. Tripād means spiritual objects, spiritual place, and spiritual instruments which cause bliss. Or it may mean the deities who act as the Lord’s ornaments and weapons in this world, the nitya-siddhas and the jīvas who become liberated.

### 3.12.7

*yad vai tad brahmetīdaṁ vāva tad yo 'yaṁ bahirdhā puruñād ākāśo yo vai sa bahirdhā puruñād ākāśaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**That which is Brahman is the ākāśa outside the body.**

**Комментарий:**

In order to praise the heart as the object of worship, having four parts of Brahman, the external ākāśa is equated with Brahman in order to glorify the ākāśa in the heart, by stating nondifference with the external ākāśa. Similarity to Brahman is stated since ākāśa spreads everywhere, is without form and is without movement. The external ākāśa which is Brahman is the ākāśa situated within the body.

### 3.12.8

*ayaṁ vāva sa yo 'yam antaḥ puruñe ākāśo yo vai so 'ntaḥ puruña ākāśaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**That external ākāśa is the ākāśa within the body.**

**Комментарий:**

The external ākāśa which is Brahman is the ākāśa situated within the body. Thought there is different between the two ākāśas, nondifference is stated because the dharmī (Lord within) is only one.

### 3.12.9

*ayaṁ vāva sa yo 'yam antar hṛdaya ākāśas tad etat pūrṇam apravarti pūrṇam apravartinīṁ+ śriyaṁ labhate ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The ākāśa within the body is the ākāśa within the heart. That ākāśa is perfect and unchanging. He who knows this attains unlimited wealth (of liberation).**

**Комментарий:**

The ākāśa in the heart is thus praised. By praising this, there is praise for the heart which is the fourth pāda of Brahman. To remove the doubt that Brahman would be limited by these divisions, it is stated that Brahman is perfect and unchanging. By worship of Brahman one attains liberation.

## 13 - Meditation on the guardians of the doors

### 3.13.1

*tasya ha vā etasya hṛdayasya pañca deva-suñayaḥ sa yo 'sya prāṇ suñiḥ sa prāṇas tac cakñuḥ sa ādityas tad etat tejo 'nnādyam ity upāsīta tejasvy annādo bhavati ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The heart, the fourth aspect of Brahman, has five doors with presiding deities. The eastern door is prāṇa, the eye, the sun. One should meditate on the sun, the doorkeeper, as power and enjoyment of food. He who knows this attains power and enjoyment of food.**

**Комментарий:**

Worship of the doors as an aṅga of worship of just described is recommended. The sun gives power to the eye which is nourished by prāṇa. One should meditate on the door keeper, the sun as power and enjoyment of food.

### 3.13.2

*atha yo 'sya dakñiṇaḥ suñiḥ sa vyānas tac chrotraṁ+ sa candramās tad etac chrīś ca yaśaś cety upāsīta śrī-mān yaśasvī bhavati ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The southern door is vyāna, the ear, the moon. One should meditate on the moon as wealth and fame. He who knows this attains wealth and fame.**

### 3.13.3

*atha yo 'sya pratyaṅ suñiḥ so 'pānaḥ sā vāk so 'gnis tad etad brahma-varcasam annādyam ity upāsīta brahma-varcasy annādo bhavati ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The western door is apāna, the voice, the fire. One should meditate on Agni as knowledge of Vedas and enjoyment of food. He who knows this attains knowledge of the Vedas and enjoyment of food.**

### 3.13.4

*atha yo 'syodaṅ suñiḥ sa samānas tan manaḥ sa parjanyas tad etad kīrtiś ca vyuñṭiś cety upāsīta kīrti-mān vyuñṭi-mān bhavati ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The northern door is samāna, the mind, Parjanya. One should meditate on the Parjanya as fame and physical beauty. He who knows this attains fame and physical beauty.**

### 3.13.5

*atha yo 'syordhvaḥ suñiḥ sa udānaḥ sa vāyuḥ sa ākāśas tad etad ojaś ca mahaś cety upāsītaujasvī mahas-vān bhavati ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The upwards door is udāna, air and ākāśa. One should meditate on ākāśa as strength and glory. He who knows this attains strength and glory.**

**Комментарий:**

Ākāśa is a particular devatā.

### 3.13.6

*te vā ete pañca brahma-puruñāḥ svargasya lokasya dvāra-pāḥ sa ya etān evaṁ pañca brahma-puruñān svargasya lokasya dvāra-pān vedāsya kule viro jāyate*  
*pratipadyate svargaṁ lokaṁ ya etan evaṁ pañca brahma-puruñan svargasya lokasya dvārapān veda*

**Перевод:**

**These five spiritual figures are the door keepers of Svarga-loka. He who worships these five puruñas, the door keepers of Svarga-loka, obtains valiant sons. He who worships the five puruñas, doorkeepers of Svargaloka, attains the abode of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

He obtains sons who are strong with knowledge. This is the secondary result. The main result is described. Having worshipped the doorkeepers of the Lord’s planet, he attains the Lord’s planet without obstruction. Svarga-loka here means the abode of the Lord.

### 3.13.7

*atha yad ataḥ paro divo jyotir dīpyate viśvataḥ pṛñṭheñu sarvataḥ pṛñṭheñv anuttameñūttameñu lokeñv idaṁ vāva tad yad idam asminn antaḥ puruñe jyotiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**That light (the Lord) who shines in the high sky, among the highest worlds to which nothing is higher, among individuals and collectively, is the light within the human body. That should be seen.**

**Комментарий:**

The meditation on the fire in the stomach is described in order to gain special results of beauty and fame. Viñva indicates the individual jīvas and sarva indicates the totality of jīvas. Jyoti means the supreme Lord, since he is radiant. Yasya bhāsā sarvam idaṁ vibhāti: (Muṇḍaka Upaniñad) the word yat refers to Brahman of three parts.

*ahaṁ vaiśvānaro bhūtvā prāṇināṁ deham āśritaḥ \|*  
*prāṇāpāna-samāyuktaḥ pacāmy annaṁ catur-vidham \|*

**Becoming the fire of digestion, I take shelter within all beings and digest the four types of food using the prāṇa and apāna. BG** [**15.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/15.14)

One should perceive the Lord in that fire.

### 3.13.8

*tasyaiñā dṛñṭir yatraitad asmiñ charīre saṁsparśenoñṇimānaṁ vijānāti tasyaiñā śrutir yatraitat karṇāv apigṛhya ninandam iva nadathur ivāgner iva jvalata upaśṛṇoti tad etad dṛñṭaṁ ca śrutaṁ cety upāsīta cakñuñyaḥ śruto bhavati ya evaṁ veda ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**One perceives this when one feels warmth in the body by touching, when one blocks the ears and hears sound like the rumbling of chariot wheels, like the blazing of fire. That light should be the object of meditation, being seen and heard. He who meditates in this way becomes the object of others’ vision (beauty) and becomes celebrated.**

**Комментарий:**

Cakñuñyaḥ means beautiful and śrutaḥ means famous.

## 14 - Sāṇḍilya-vidyā

### 3.14.1

*sarvaṁ khalv idaṁ brahma taj-jalān iti śānta upāsīta. atha khalu kratu-mayaḥ puruño yathā kratur asmil+ loke puruño bhavati tathetaḥ pretya bhavati sa kratuṁ kurvīta.*

**Перевод:**

**All this is Brahman. It arises from Brahman, merges in Brahman and lives in Brahman. As a man worships in this world, on dying he becomes similar in the next world. One should thus meditate on Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Sāṇḍilya-vidyā is explained. Jalān is explained as follows. Ja stands for birth. La stands for destruction. An means “lives.” Sarva means “has everything as his body.” Idam can modify sarvam or brahma. All this is brahma or everything is this. One should worship, being peaceful, thinking “All this is the body of Brahman, since everything arises from Brahman, merges into Brahman and lives in Brahman.”

The meditation is praised. Taṁ yathā yathopāsate tathaiva bhavati: according to worship one attains the result. (Śatapaṭha-brāhmaṇa) Because he worships (puruñaḥ kratumayaḥ) he attains the result. It must be done since it is most beneficial.

### 3.14.2

*mano-mayaḥ prāṇa-śarīro bhā-rūpaḥ satya-saṅkalpa ākāśātmā sarva-karmā sarva-kāmaḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idam abhyātto 'vāky anādaraḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**He is realized by the pure mind. He supports all prāṇas. He has a radiant form. His desires are always fulfilled. He is pure and fine like ākāśa. He creates the universe. He has all enjoyable objects. He has spiritual fragrance and taste. He accepts all these qualities. He is silent and completely detached from the material world.**

**Комментарий:**

The qualities of Brahman are described.

The Lord can be realized only by the pure mind (manomayaḥ), purified by meditation with seven sādhanas. He maintains all the prāṇas in the universe. He has a radiant form (bhā-rūpaḥ) filled with most auspicious, extraordinary spiritual qualities. His desire or will is never obstructed (satya-saṅkalpaḥ). His svarūpa is pure and fine like ākāśah (ākāśātmā). He is the ātmā of the avyākṛta ākāśa which is the cause of all else. He manifests by himself and manifests all others (ākāśātmā). He produces the universe (sarva-karmā). He possesses all pure objects and instruments of enjoyment. Aśabdhaṁ asparśam: he is free of material sound and touch. Thus he is endowed with spiritual fragrance and spiritual taste, free of all faults and endowed with extraordinary qualities. He accepts all these auspicious qualities (abhyāttaḥ).

He does not speak (avakī) since he has no interest in anything in this material world from Brahmā down to grass, because all desires are fulfilled and he has all powers in full.

### 3.14.3

*eña ma ātmāntar hṛdaye 'ṇīyān vrīher vā yavād vā sarñapād vā śāmakād vā śyāmaka-taṇḍulād vā eña ma ātmāntar hṛdaye jyāyān pṛthivyā jyāyān antarikñāj jyāyān divo jyāyān ebhyo lokebhyaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord in my heart is smaller than a grain of rice, a grain of barely, a mustard seed or a grain of millet. The Lord in my heart is greater that the earth, greater than the antarikña, greater than heaven, greater than these planets.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is situated in the heart in a small form for meditation. One should meditate on this form. However, though he is within the heart, one should also meditate on his huge form, his very svarūpa.

### 3.14.4

*sarva-karmā sarva-kāmaḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idam abhyātto 'vāky anādaraḥ eña ma ātmāntar hṛdaya etad brahma etam itaḥ pretyābhisambhavitāsmi yasya syād addhā na vicikitsāstīti sa smāha śāṇḍilyaḥ śāṇḍilyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord is the creator of the universe. He has all enjoyable objects. He has spiritual fragrance and taste. He accepts all these auspicious qualities. He is without material speech. He is disinterested in the material world. He is in my heart. He is Brahman. After dying I will attain the Lord. For a person who thinks in this way, there will certainly be no doubt. This is the statement of Śaṇḍilya.**

**Комментарий:**

That supreme Brahman, the Lord, by his great mercy, dwells in my heart with a desire to deliver me (eña ma ātmāntar hṛdaye). One should meditate on the Lord as the person who will bring one to a special place on leaving the material body. Paraṁ jyoti-rūpa-saṁpadya svena rupenābhiniñpadyate: rising from his body, he attains the supreme Lord and manifests with his own form. (Chāndogya Upaniñad) To gain faith in the statements, the attainment of the speaker is shown.

## 15 - Mediation on the treasure chest

### 3.15.1

*antarikñodaraḥ kośo bhūmi-budhno na jīryate diśo hy asya sraktayo dyaur asyottaraṁ bilaṁ+ sa eña kośo vasudhānas tasmin viśvam idaṁ+ śritam.*

**Перевод:**

**The treasure chest, having antarikña as the belly and earth as its bottom, does not decay. The direction are its corners. The heaven is its upper opening. This chest stores the results of karma. All the results of karma take shelter of this chest.**

**Комментарий:**

Knowledge of the treasury made of the three worlds, for giving long life to sons, is introduced. The antarikña is the belly (udaram) since it is in the middle. It is like a treasure chest. Earth is its bottom. It is not destroyed (na jīryati). That means it remains for a long time. The directions are its corners. The heaven is its upper opening. This chest stores the agents and the results of karma (vasudhānaḥ). All the results of karma take shelter of this chest.

### 3.15.2

*tasya prācī dig juhūr nāma sahamānā nāma dakñiṇā rājñī nāma pratīcī subhūtā nāmodīcī tāsāṁ vāyur vatsaḥ sa ya etam evaṁ vāyuṁ diśaṁ vatsa veda na putra-rodaṁ+ roditi so 'ham etam evaṁ vāyuṁ diśāṁ vatsaṁ veda mā putra-rodaṁ rudam.*

**Перевод:**

**The eastern direction is called Juhu. The South side is called Sahamāna. The west side is called Rajñī. The north side is called Subhūtā. Vāyu is their son. He who knows that Vāyu is the son of the directions does not cry for the loss of a son. “I know that Vāyu is the son of the directions. May I not cry for the loss of a son!”**

### 3.15.3

*ariñṭaṁ kośaṁ prapadye 'munāmunāmunā prāṇaṁ prapadye 'munāmunāmunā bhūḥ prapadye 'munāmunāmunā bhuvaḥ prapadye 'munāmunāmunā svaḥ prapadye 'munāmunāmunā.*

**Перевод:**

**I surrender to the indestructible chest, for that son, for that son, for that son. I surrender to prāṇa for that son, for that son, for that son. I surrender to earth for that son, for that son, for that son. I surrender to bhuvaḥ for that son, for that son, for that son. I surrender to svaḥ for that, for that son, for that son.**

**Комментарий:**

Mantras of surrender which are aṅgas of knowledge of the chest are given. Amunā means for that son.

### 3.15.4

*sa yad avocaṁ prāṇaṁ prapadya iti prāṇo vā idaṁ+ sarvaṁ bhūtaṁ yad idaṁ kiñca tam eva tat prāpatsi.*

**Перевод:**

**When I said that I surrender to prāṇa it means, “I have surrendered to the universe, all that exists, made of prāṇa.”**

**Комментарий:**

The previous mantras are explained.

### 3.15.5

*atha yad avocaṁ bhūḥ prapadya iti prṭhivīṁ prapadye 'ntarikñaṁ prapady divaṁ prapadya it eva tad avocam.*

**Перевод:**

**When I said I surrender to earth I said, “I have surrendered to the earth, I surrender to the antarikña, I surrender to the heaven.”**

**Комментарий:**

I surrender to earth means I surrender to the three worlds.

### 3.15.6

*atha yad avocaṁ bhuvaḥ prapadya ity agniṁ prapadye vāyuṁ prapadya ādityaṁ prapadya ity eva tad avocam.*

**Перевод:**

**When I said I surrender to bhuvaḥ I said, “I surrender to Agni, I surrender to the Vāyu, I surrender to Āditya.”**

### 3.15.7

*atha yad avocaṁ+ svaḥ prapadya ity ṛg-vedaṁ prapadye yajur-vedaṁ prapadye sāma-vedaṁ prapadya ity eva tad avocaṁ tad avocam.*

**Перевод:**

**When I said I surrender to svaḥ I said, “I surrender to Rg Veda, I surrender to Yajur Veda, I surrender to Sāma Veda.”**

## 16 - Puruña-vidyā for long life

### 3.16.1

*puruño vāva yajñas tasya yāni catur-viṁ+śati-varñāṇi tat prātaḥ-savanaṁ catur-viṁ+śaty-akñarā gāyatrī gāyatraṁ prātaḥ-savanaṁ tad asya vasavo 'nyvāyattāḥ prāṇā vāva vasava ete hīdaṁ+ sarvaṁ vāsayanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The man is yajña. Twenty-four years is the morning libation. Gāyatrī meter has twenty-four syllables. Gāyatrī is chanted during the morning period. The Vasus are the presiding deities of this period. Prāṇas are the Vasus. The prāṇas caused everything to shine.**

**Комментарий:**

Puruña-vidyā to attain long life is introduced. The prāṇas are considered to be the Vasus, since the healthy condition of the body is dependent on prāṇas.

### 3.16.2

*taṁ ced etasmin vayasi kiñcid upatapet sa brūyāt prāṇā vasava idaṁ me prātaḥ-savanaṁ mādhyan-dinaṁ savanam anusantanuteti māhaṁ prāṇānāṁ vasūnāṁ madhye yajño vilopsīyety ud dhaiva tata ety agado ha bhavati.*

**Перевод:**

**If sickness afflicts him during this period he should say, “O prāṇas! O Vasus! Make my morning libation extend till the midday libation! May I, the sacrifice, not perish amidst the prāṇas and Vasus!” He becomes free of the sickness. He becomes free of disease.**

### 3.16.3

*atha yāni catuś catvāriṁśad varñāṇi tan mādhyan-dinaṁ+ savanaṁ catuś catvāriṁśad akñarā triñṭup traiñṭubhaṁ mādhyan-dinaṁ+ savanaṁ tad asya rudrā anvāyattāḥ prāṇā vāva rudrā ete hīdaṁ+ sarvaṁ+ rodayanti.*

**Перевод:**

**The next forty-four years are the midday libation. The triñṭup meter has forty-four syllables. Triñṭup verses are recited during the midday libation. Rudras are the deities. Prāṇas are the Rudras. They make everyone weep.**

### 3.16.4

*taṁ ced etasmin vayasi kiñcid upatapet sa brūyāt prāṇā rudrā idaṁ me mādhyan-dinaṁ+ savanaṁ tṛtīya-savanam anusantanuteti māhaṁ prāṇānāṁ+ rudrāṇāṁ madhye yajño vilopsīyet ud dhaiva tata ety agado ha bhavati.*

**Перевод:**

**If sickness afflicts him during this period he should say, “O prāṇas! O Rudras! Make my midday libation extend till the third libation! May I, the sacrifice, not perish amidst the prāṇas and Rudras!” He becomes free of the sickness. He becomes free of disease.**

### 3.16.5

*atha yāny añṭā-catvāriṁ+śad varñāṇi tat tṛtīya-savanam añṭā-catvāriṁ+śad akñarā jagatī jāgataṁ tṛtīya-savanaṁ tad asyādityā anvāyattāḥ prāṇā vāvādityā ete hīdaṁ+ sarvam ādadate.*

**Перевод:**

**The next forty-eight years are the third libation. The jagati meter has forty-eight syllables. Jagati verses are recited during the third libation. Ādityas are the deities. Prāṇas are the ādityas. They receive all this.**

### 3.16.6

*taṁ ced etasmin vayasi kiñcid upatapet sa brūyāt prāṇā ādityā idaṁ me tṛtīya-savanam āyur anusantanuteti māhaṁ prāṇānām ādityānāṁ madhye yajño vilopsīyet ud dhaiva tata ety agado haiva bhavati.*

**Перевод:**

**If sickness afflicts him during this period he should say, “O prāṇas! O ādityas! Make my third libation extend for my whole life! May I, the sacrifice, not perish amidst the prāṇas and ādityas!” He becomes free of the sickness. He becomes free of disease.**

### 3.16.7

*etad dha sma vai tad vidvān āha mahidāsa aitareyaḥ sa kiṁ ma etad upatapasi yo 'ham anena na preñyāmīti sa ha ñoḍaśaṁ varña-śatam ajīvat pra ha ñoḍaśaṁ varña-śataṁ jīvati ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**Mahidāsa Aitareya, knowing this, said, “Why do you afflict me? I will not die from this affliction.” He lived for a hundred and sixteen years. He who knows this lives for a hundred and sixteen years.**

## 17 - Similarity of man to a sacrifice

### 3.17.1

*sa yad aśiśiñati yat pipāsati yan na ramate tā asya dīkñā.*

**Перевод:**

**When a person desires to eat, desires to drink and does not enjoy, that is dīkñā for performing sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

This is considered to be dīkñā in the sacrifice because these conditions are full of suffering.

### 3.17.2

*atha yad aśnāti yat pibati yad ramate tad upasadair eti.*

**Перевод:**

**When one eats, drinks and enjoys it is similar to the upasada.**

**Комментарий:**

The enjoyments are similar to the upasada with payovrata (rituals in which one can eat) as it also gives pleasure.

### 3.17.3

*atha yad dhasati ya jakñati yan maithunaṁ carati stuta-śastrair eva tad eti.*

**Перевод:**

**When a person laughs, eats and enjoys sex, it is similar to the hymns and scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

The sounds are similar to the chanting of hymns.

### 3.17.4

*atha yat tapo dānam ārjavam ahiṁ+sā saya-vacanam iti tā asya dakñiṇāḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**Austerity, charity, honesty, nonviolence and truthfulness are the fees.**

**Комментарий:**

These items are similar to the fees since they nourish dharma.

### 3.17.5

*tasmād āhuḥ soñyaty asoñṭeti punar utpādanam evāsya tan maraṇam evāsyāvabhṛtaḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**They say “He extracts the soma.” Similarly the mother gives birth to a person. This is like the birth of the person. The avabhrta is like a person’s death.**

**Комментарий:**

Man is like a sacrifice. When a person is about to perform sacrifice someone says somaṁ soñyati: he will extract soma. Similarly it is said the mother gave birth (asoñṭa). Thus this is like birth.

### 3.17.6

*tad dhaitad ghora āṅgirasaḥ kṛñṇāya devakī-putrāyoktvovācāpipāsa eva sa babhūva so 'nta-velāyām etat trayaṁ pratipadyetākñitam asy acyutam asi prāṇa-saṁ+śitam asīti tatraite dve ṛcau bhavataḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**Ghora Āṅgirasa, having performed the sacrifice in this manner (meditating on it as a person) for Kṛñṇa, son of Devakī, became free of thirst. One who at the time of death utters the three statements also becomes free of thirst. “You are without decay. You are unchanging. You are the subtle life of the universe.” There are two ṛg mantras concerning this.**

**Комментарий:**

A person named Ghora of the Aṅgirasa gotra, seeing the sacrifice as a person (tad etat), performed it (uvāca) for (uktvā) the pleasure of Kṛñṇa. Attaining brahma-vidyā by that, he became free of thirst or liberated. Though the sacrifice seen as a human gives a hundred and sixteen years of life, when practiced with the Lord as goal it yields brahma-vidyā. He who utters the three statements also becomes free of thirst.

He should chant these mantras. You are devoid of deterioration (akñitam asi). You are devoid of change in your svarūpa and svabhāva. You are a subtle entity giving life to the universe (prāṇa-saṁsitan asi). On that subject of Parabrahman there are two Rg mantras.

### 3.17.7

*Ādit pratnasya retasa udvayam tamasas pari jyotiḥ paśyanta uttaraṁ svaḥ paśyanta uttaraṁ devaṁ devatrā sūryam aganma jyotir uttamam iti jyotir uttamam.*

**Перевод:**

**The devotees see the Lord, who is the basis of the most ancient seed of the universe. Seeing the supreme Lord beyond prakṛti, seeing the excellent spiritual world, we have attained that supreme Lord, shining among all shining objects, who is attained by the path of the sun.**

**Комментарий:**

The first mantra is summarized as ādit-pratnasya retasa. The complete mantra is Ādit-pratnasya retaso jyotiñ paśyanti vāsaram paro ya didhyate divi. The eternal devotees see the supreme Brahman (jyotiḥ), the basis (ādit) of the ancient (pratnasya) seed of the universe (retasaḥ), avyakta. Sadā paśyanti surayaḥ: the devotees always see that place. The Lord is called jyoti or light because he removes all darkness of saṁsāra. He has a form which is eternally manifested (vāsaram), which shines (didhyate) in the spiritual sky (divi).

We see the supreme (uttaram) light (Lord) who is beyond (pari) prakṛti (tamasa). We see the spiritual sky (svaḥ), where we see the Lord. Paśyanta stands for paśyantaḥ (seeing). We (vayam) attained (udaganma) the excellent (uttaram) shining (deva) form among all shining forms (devatrā), which is attained by the path of the sun (sūryam).

### 18

### 3.18.1

*mano brahmety upasītety adhyātmam athādhidaivatam ākāśo brahmety ubhayam ādiñṭaṁ bhavaty adhyātmaṁ cādhidaivataṁ ca.*

**Перевод:**

**One should meditate on the mind as Brahman. This is related to the body. One should meditate on ākāśa as Brahman. This is related to the devatās. Both these methods, related to one’s body and to the devatās, are taught.**

### 3.18.2

*tad etac catuñ-pād brahma vāk pādaḥ prāṇaḥ pādaś cakñuḥ pādaḥ śrotraṁ pāda ity adhyātmam athādhidaivatam agniḥ pādo vāyuḥ pāda ādityaḥ pādo diśaḥ pāda ity ubhayam evādiñṭaṁ bhavaty adhyātmaṁ caivādhidaivataṁ ca.*

**Перевод:**

**Brahman has four pādas: voice is one pāda, prāṇa is another pāda. The eye is another pāda. The ear is another pāda. These are related to the body. Now the pādas related to the deities are described. Agni is one pāda. Vāyu is another pāda, āditya is another pāda. The direction is the last pāda. Both these, related to the body and the devatās are taught.**

**Комментарий:**

Brahman or the mind has four pādas. As Brahman has four parts so the mind has four parts. Since voice etc. are controlled by the mind they are said to be four pādas of the mind. Brahman as ākāśa has four parts: fire, air, the sun and the directions.

### 3.18.3

*vāg eva brahmaṇaś caturthaḥ pādaḥ so 'gninā jyotiñā bhāti ca tapati ca bhāti ca tapati ca kīrtyā yaśasā brahma-vacasena ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**Voice is the fourth pāda of Brahman. That pāda shines and is enthusiastic to perform its function by the light of fire. He who knows this shines and is enthusiastic to perform his function with fame, renown and knowledge of Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Tapati means “it is enthusiastic to perform its function.” Agni is the deity controlling the voice. Agni gives speech its power to reveal things. Kirti is fame from charity. Yaśa is fame from valor. Brahma varcasā is increased amount of studies.

### 3.18.4

*prāṇa eva brahmaṇaś caturthaḥ pādaḥ sa vāyunā jyotiñā bhāti ca tapati ca bhāti ca tapati ca kīrtyā yaśasā brahma-vacasena ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**Prāṇa is the fourth pāda of Brahman. That pāda shines and is enthusiastic to perform its function by the light of air. He who knows this shines and is enthusiastic to perform his function with fame, renown and knowledge of Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Prāṇa and air are considered to the same.

### 3.18.5

*cakñur eva brahmaṇaś caturthaḥ pādaḥ sa ādityena jyotiñā bhāti ca tapati ca bhāti ca tapati ca kīrtyā yaśasā brahma-vacasena ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The eye is the fourth pāda of Brahman. That pāda shines and is enthusiastic to perform its function by the light of the sun. He who knows this shines and is enthusiastic to perform his function with fame, renown and knowledge of Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

The sun is the deity of the eye.

### 3.18.6

*śrotram eva brahmaṇaś caturthaḥ pādaḥ sa digbhir jyotiñā bhāti ca tapati ca bhāti ca tapati ca kīrtyā yaśasā brahma-vacasena ya evaṁ veda ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**The ear is the fourth pāda of Brahman. That pāda shines and is enthusiastic to perform its function by the light of the directions. He who knows this shines and is enthusiastic to perform his function with fame, renown and knowledge of Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

The directions are the deity of the ear.

## 19 - Āditya as Brahman

### 3.19.1

*ādityo brahmetyādeśas tasyopavyākhyānam asad evedam agra āsīt. tat sad āsīt tat samabhavat tadāṇḍaṁ niravartata tat saṁvatsarasya mātrām aśayata tan nirabhidyata te āṇḍa-kapāle rajataṁ ca suvarṇaṁ cābhavatām.*

**Перевод:**

**Āditya is Brahman. This is the teaching. It is explained as follows. In the beginning the universe was unmanifested. It then became slightly manifested. It assumed a gross form. It became an egg. It remained in that state for a year. Then it split. The two halves were silver and gold.**

**Комментарий:**

Previously Aditya was one pāda of Brahman. Now it is regarded as the whole Brahman.

In the beginning this universe (idam) was without names and forms (asat). That universe became slightly inclined to manifest name and form (sat asīt). The seed gradually became gross (tat samabhavat), accepting names and forms. Then it developed a covering like an egg (aṇḍam). It remained in this condition (aśayata) for a year. After one year the egg split. The two egg shells became silver and gold.

### 3.19.2

*tad rajataṁ seyaṁ pṛthivī yat suvarṇaṁ sā dyaur yaj jarāyu te parvatā yad ulbaṁ+ sa megho nīhāro yā dhamanayas tā nadyo yad vāsteyam udakaṁ+ sa samudraḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The lower silver half was the earth. The upper gold half was heaven. The surrounding gross membrane was the mountains. The fine membrane was clouds and frost. The arteries were the rivers. The fluid in the bladder was the ocean.**

### 3.19.3

*atha yat tad ajāyata so 'sāv ādityas taṁ jāyamānaṁ ghoñā ulūlavo 'nūdatiñṭhan sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni ca sarve ca kāmās tasmāt tasyodayaṁ prati tasyāyanaṁ prati ghoñā ulūlavo 'nūttiñṭhanti sarvāṇi ca bhūtāni sarve caiva kāmāḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**What arose in that womb was āditya. When āditya was born shouts and loud clamor arose.**

**All beings and enjoyables arose at that time. Thus all beings and enjoyables made loud noises at the rising and setting of the sun.**

### 3.19.4

*sa ya etam evaṁ vidvān ādityaṁ brahmety upāste 'bhyāśo ha yad enaṁ+ sādhavo ghoñā ā ca gaccheyur upa ca nimreḍeran nimreḍeran.*

**Перевод:**

**To the person who, knowing this, meditates on the sun as Brahman, auspicious sounds come and cause him pleasure.**

## Part 4

## 1 - Saṁvarga-vidyā

### 4.1.1

*oṁ jānaśrutir ha pautrāyaṇaḥ śraddhādeyo bahu-dāyī bahu-pākya āsa sa ha sarvata āvasathān māpayāṁ cakre sarva eva me 'annam atsyantīti.*

**Перевод:**

**Jānaśruti, the grandson of Putra, with faith in giving, inclined to give charity in plenty, cooking plenty of food in his house and build shelters everywhere so that all could eat his food.**

**Комментарий:**

Samvarga-vidyā is introduced. A story is told to praise the vidyā. Janaśruta’s son was Jānaśruti. He was the grandson of Putra. He gave gifts with great trust (śraddhādeyaḥ). He gave charity to guests in plenty. He cooked plenty of food in his house (bahu-pākyaḥ). He built shelters everywhere filled with food, water, beds, furniture to prevent cold, wind, rain and sun for travelers without protection, in villages, towns, on roads and in forests so that guests could eat his food.

### 4.1.2

*atha ha haṁ+sā niśāyām atipetus tad dhaivaṁ+ haṁ+sam abhyuvāda bho bho 'yi bhallākña bhallākña jānaśruteḥ pautrāyaṇasya samaṁ divā jyotir ātataṁ tan mā prasāṅkñīs tat tvā mā pradhākñīr iti.*

**Перевод:**

**One night some swans flew past his house. One swan said to another, “O Bhallākña! Do not go near the effulgence of Jānaśruti, grandson of Putra, which is bright like daylight. It should not burn you.**

**Комментарий:**

One night, some great souls, satisfied with his giving food, taking the form of swans, became visible to the king. At that time (tat) one swan, coming from behind, spoke to a swan flying in front.

O Bhallākña (short-sighted one)! He repeats is name out of respect. Do not come near the effulgence emanating from the king which is like the day or like Svarga (diva samam). That effulgence should not burn you (pradhākñīt).

### 4.1.3

*tam u ha paraḥ pratyuvāca kaṁ vara enam etat santaṁ+ sayugvānam iva raikvam āttheti yo nu kathaṁ sayugvā raikka iti.*

**Перевод:**

**The other bird answered. “Why do you praise Jānaśruti like Raikva with the cart?” The other bird answered, “Who is Raikva with the cart?”**

**Комментарий:**

Hearing the words praising the king, another bird could not tolerate and answered. Raikva knows Brahman and has great power. What is the power of this person devoid of knowledge of Brahman? Can he burn me up? The swan behind asked, “What type of person is that person whom you described?”

### 4.1.4

*yathā kṛtāya-vijitāyādherayāḥ saṁyanty evam enaṁ+ sarvaṁ tad abhisamaiti yat kiñca prajāḥ sādhu kurvanti yas tad veda yat sa veda sa mayaitad ukta iti.*

**Перевод:**

**When a person wins at dice with kata (four), all the lesser numbers are included in it. Similarly whatever good deeds people perform go to Raikva. His knowledge includes whatever anyone else knows. This is the person I mentioned.**

### 4.1.5

*tad u ha jānaśrutiḥ pautrāyaṇa upaśuśrāva sa ha sañjihāna eva kñattāram uvācāṅgāre ha sayugvānam iva raikkam āttheti yo nu kathaṁ+ sayugvā raikka iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Jānaśruti, grandson of Putra, overheard the conversation. Rising in the morning, he said to his doorkeeper, “O sir! The swan said, ‘You praise the king as if he were Raikva with the cart.’ ‘What type of person is Raikva with cart?’ replied the other swan.”**

**Комментарий:**

Jānaśruti heard the praise of Raikva, which hinted at criticism of himself. Rising after the night he spoke to his doorkeeper. Kñatta is a doorkeeper of pratiloma birth. He repeated to him the conversation.

### 4.1.6

*yathā kṛtāya-vijitāyādhareyāḥ saṁyanty evam enaṁ+ sarvaṁ tad abhisamaiti sat kiñca prajāḥ sādhu kurvanti yad tad veda yat sa veda sa mayaitad ukta iti.*

**Перевод:**

**When a person wins at dice with kata (four), all the lesser numbers are included in it. Similarly whatever good deeds people perform go to Raikva. His knowledge includes whatever anyone else knows. This is the person I mentioned.**

### 4.1.7

*sa ha kñattānviñya nāvidam iti pratyeyāya taṁ+ hovāca yatrāre brāhmaṇasyānveñaṇā tad enam arccheti.*

**Перевод:**

**The doorkeeper returned and said, “I did not find him.” The king said, “Go and search for him where knowers of Brahman reside.”**

**Комментарий:**

The doorkeeper, remembering the characteristics of Raikva with the cart, searched from him in the towns and villages but could not find him. He then returned. Go and search for him in lonely places, in forests and on river banks.

### 4.1.8

*so 'dhastāc chakaṭasya pāmānaṁ kañamānam upopaviveśa taṁ+ hābhyuvāda tvaṁ nu bhagavaḥ sayugvā raikva ity ahaṁ+ hy arā3 iti ha pratijajñe sa ha kñattāvidam iti pratyeyāya.*

**Перевод:**

**He sat near a man beneath a cart who was scratching his sores. He said to him, “Are you Raikva with the cart?” He answered, “I am.” The doorkeeper returned to the king and said, “I have learned of him.”**

### 2

### 4.2.1

*tad u ha jānaśrutiḥ pautrāyaṇaḥ ñaṭ śatāni gavāṁ niñkam āśvatarī-rathaṁ tadādāya praticakrame taṁ+ hābhyuvāda.*

**Перевод:**

**Jānaśruti, grandson of Putra, taking six hundred cows, a necklace and a chariot drawn by mules, went to Raikva and spoke.**

### 4.2.2

*raikvemāni ñaṭ śatāni gavām ayaṁ niñko 'yam aśvatarī-ratho nu ma etāṁ bhagavo devatāṁ+ śādhi yāṁ devatām upāsse iti.*

**Перевод:**

**O Raikva! Accept the six hundred cows, the necklace and cart with mules that I have brought. Dear sir! Please inform me of the devatā that you worship.**

### 4.2.3

*tam u ha paraḥ pratyuvācāha hāretvā śudra tavaiva saha gobhir astv iti tad u ha punar eva jānaśrutiḥ pautrāyaṇaḥ sahasraṁ gavāṁ niñkam āśvatī-rathaṁ duhitaraṁ tad ādāya praticakrame.*

**Перевод:**

**Raikva replied to him, “O sūdra! Keep your cows along with the necklace and cart with mules.” Jānaśruti again came with a thousand cows the necklace and the cart with mules as well as his daughter.**

**Комментарий:**

Raikva, who knew the three worlds by the power of yoga, understood that Jānaśruti was qualified for inquiry about Brahman after his grief on hearing the swan indicate his lack of brahma-jñāna. Raikva thought that though one is qualified for brahma-vidyā, even if qualified, without service for a long time, and even if serving, without giving wealth according to one’s full capacity, brahma-vidyā cannot be established. Being merciful to him, in order to increase his determination for inquiry about Brahman, through an abbreviated means to indicate Jānaśruti’s lament, he addressed him as śūdra to indicate that lamentation. Let the cows as well as the necklace (hāra) and the cart with mules (itvā) stay with you. How can I maintain the cows without a house and wife? How can you be fixed in brahma-vidyā by giving a meager amount of my maintenance? He brought more cows and his daughter to the village for marriage with Raikva.

### 4.2.4

*taṁ hābhyuvāda raikvedaṁ+ sahasraṁ gavām ayaṁ niñko 'yam aśvatarī-ratha iyaṁ jāyā’yaṁ grāmo yasminn āsse 'nv eva mā bhagavaḥ śādhīti.*

**Перевод:**

**He said to him, “O Raikva! Here are a thousand cows, a necklace, and a cart with mules. This is the village where you will live. Sir! Teach me.”**

**Комментарий:**

What you have accepted is everything I have. Teach me and do not refuse.”

### 4.2.5

*tasyā ha mukham upodgṛhṇann uvācājahāremāḥ śūdrānenaiva mukhenālāpayiñyathā iti te haite raikka-parṇā mahā-vṛñeñu yatrāsmā uvāsa tasmai hovāca.*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting his gifts, Raikva said, “O śudra! Accepting this gift, you make me speak about Brahman, by this gift.” These are the villages known as Raikva-parṇa in the place called Mahā-vṛña, where Raikva spoke. Raikva then spoke.**

**Комментарий:**

He again addressed him as śūdra, when offering to teach him about Brahman. Thus śūdra does not mean the fourth varṇa, since one should teach the fourth varṇa about Brahman.

These were the villages given by Jānaśruti to Raikva. Raikva taught him about the Lord that he worshipped.

### 3

### 4.3.1

*vāyur vāha saṁvargo yadā vā agnir udvāyati vāyum evāpyeti yadā sūryo 'stam eti vāyum evāpyeti yadā cendro 'stam eti vāyum evāpyeti.*

**Перевод:**

**They say that air is absorption. When fire is extinguished it merges with air alone. When the sun sets, it merges with air alone. When the moon sets it merges with air alone.**

**Комментарий:**

Vāyu or air is saṁvarga (absorption) because of its quality of absorbing into itself. Saṁvarga means gathering together, making one. One should think of Vāyu as having this quality of absorbing. That is now taught. Udvāyatyi means to be destroyed.

### 4.3.2

*yadāpa ucchuñyanti vāyum evāpiyanti vāyur hy evaitān sarvān saṁvṛṅkta ity adhidaivatam.*

**Перевод:**

**When water dries up it enters air alone. Air alone absorbs all these. This is related to devatās (of fire etc.)**

**Комментарий:**

Air is said to be the absorbing power since the light of fire, the sun and moon, and water merge into air. Since it is understood that Brahman is being praised in this section, air here means the supreme Lord, who has air as his body, in order to harmonize with the context. Saṁvṛṇkta means “become one with.”

### 4.3.3

*athādhyātmaṁ prāṇo vāva saṁvargaḥ sa yadā svapiti prāṇam eva vāg apyeti prāṇaṁ cakñuḥ prāṇaṁ śrotraṁ prāṇaṁ manaḥ prāṇo hy evaitān sarvān saṁvṛṅkta iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Now absorption in relation to the body is explained. Prāṇa is absorption. When a person sleeps, voice merges into prāṇa, the eye merges into prāṇa, the ear merges into prāṇa, and the mind merges into prāṇa. Prāṇa alone absorbs all these.**

**Комментарий:**

One should meditate on prāṇa as absorption since voice, eye, ear and mind mercy into prāṇa. Prāṇa also means the Lord, who has prāṇa as his body.

### 4.3.4

*tau vā etau dvau saṁvargau vāyur eva deveñu prāṇaḥ prāṇeñu.*

**Перевод:**

**These two are the absorption agents: air for the devatās and prāṇa for the senses.**

### 4.3.5

*atha ha śaunaka ca kāpeyam abhipratāriṇaṁ ca kākñaseniṁ pariviñyamāṇau brahma-cārī bibhikñe tasmā u ha na dadatuḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The son of Śunaka of the Kapi gotra and Ahipratārinā, son of Kakñaseni, had sat down to eat. A brahmacārī begged from them. They did not give anything.**

**Комментарий:**

A story is told in order to praise saṁvarga-vidyā. A brahmacārī, who was a worshipper of saṁvarga-vidyā begged from them. They did not give, thinking that they wanted to hear what the proud brahmacārī would say.

### 4.3.6

*sa hovāca mahātmanaś caturo deva ekaḥ kaḥ sa jagāra bhuvanasya gopās taṁ kāpeya nābhipaśyanti martyā abhipratārin bahudhā vasantaṁ yasmai vā etad annaṁ tasmā etan na dattam iti.*

**Перевод:**

**The brahmacārī spoke. “The protector of all planets, the one Lord, who is Prajāpati, devoured the four devatās and four senses. O Kapeya and Ahibrpatāri! Humans do not know the person who resides with many forms. You did not give food to that person.”**

**Комментарий:**

Kaḥ means Prajāpati. They did not give food to that all-pervading Prajāpati for whom the whole world is food. The brahmacārī thus criticized them.

### 4.3.7

*tad u ha śaunakaḥ kāpeyaḥ pratimanvānaḥ pratyeyāyātmā devānāṁ*  
*janitā prajānāṁ+ hiraṇya-daṁ+ñṭro babhaso 'nasūrir mahāntamasya mahimānam āhur anadyamāno yad annam attīti vai vayaṁ brahma-cārin nedam upāsmahe dattāsmai bhikñām iti.*

**Перевод:**

**Śaunaka of the Kapeya gotra contemplated his words. He came to him and said, “The controller of the devatās, the creator of the prajās, has attractive teeth. He kills the demons. He is intelligent. They speak of the Lord’s great powers. Not devoured by others, he devours food. O brahmacārī! We do not worship Prajāpati that you described.” They said, “Give him food.”**

**Комментарий:**

He is the controller (ātmā) of the devatās. He produces the prajās (janatā). He has beautiful teeth or has teeth that tore up Hiraṇyakaśipur (hiraṇyadaṁñtṛaḥ). He devours (babhasaḥ). This means he kills demons. He is not devoid of intelligence (anasuriḥ). They speak of the great glories of the Lord (asya), or Nṛsiṁha. We do not worship he who is not devoured by others (anadhyamānaḥ) but who destroys everything such as voice etc. The Lord is saṁvarga, not Prajāpati described by you. Thus we do not worship Prajāpati as saṁvarga, but the Lord. You do not know saṁvarga. Saying this, they instructed the servers “Give him food.” In saṁvarga-vidyā, air and prāṇa mean the supreme Lord.

### 4.3.8

*tasmā u ha dadus te vā ete pañcānye pañcānye daśa santas tat-kṛtaṁ tasmāt sarvāsu dikñv annam eva daśa kṛtaṁ+ saiñā virāḍ annādī tayedaṁ+ sarvaṁ dṛñṭaṁ+ sarvam asyedaṁ dṛñṭaṁ bhavaty annādo bhavati ya evaṁ veda ya evaṁ veda.*

**Перевод:**

**They gave him food. The five elements related to the devatās and the five elements related to the body are ten. This is the winning number in dice. This worship involving ten becomes food in the ten directions. The virāṭ meter is the eater of food. By this worship everything becomes worshipped. He who knows this becomes an eater of food.**

**Комментарий:**

The assistants gave the brahmacārī food. In order to praise saṁvarga-vidyā, elements related to saṁvarga, such as the number ten in dice, virāṭ meter, food and the eater of food are praised. The five elements related to devatās are Agni, the sun, the moon and water, and Vāyu which absorbs them. The elements related to the body are voice, eye, ear and mind and prāṇa which absorbs them. In total they are ten. This is the number of victory in dice, after totaling four, three, two and one.

Therefore the object of worship in saṁvarga-vidyā, in the form of ten in dice, is the food (objects of enjoyment) existing in the ten directions. Śruti says daśākñarā virāṭ and annaṁ virāṭ: the ten directions are virād. Food is virāṭ. The number ten, representing the saṁvarga-vidyā, also represents the eater of food, the Lord, the objects of worship in the saṁvarga-vidyā. The virāṭ meter, consisting of ten syllables is thus the eater of food (annādī), the object of worship in saṁvarga-vidyā. All objects generated in the ten directions, having similarity because of the number ten, become worshipped (dṛñṭam) by what is worshipped in the virāṭ meter. The worshipper develops all knowledge and realizes Brahman (the Lord).

## 4 - Ṣodaśa-kalā-brahma-vidyā

### 4.4.1

*satyakāmo ha jābālo jabālāṁ mātaram āmatrayāṁ cakre brahma-caryaṁ bhavati vivitsyāmi kiṁ-gotro nv aham asmīti.*

**Перевод:**

**Satyakāma, son of Jābalā asked his mother, “I desire to be a brahmacārī. What is my gotra?”**

**Комментарий:**

Śoḍaśa-kalā-brahma-idyā is introduced. Satyakāma, the son of Jabālā, asked his mother, Jabālā.

### 4.4.2

*sā hainam uvāca nāham etad veda tāta yad-gotras tvam asi bahv ahaṁ carantī paricāriṇī yauvane tvām alabhe sāham etan na veda yad-gotras tvam asi jabālā tu nāmāham asmi satyakāmo nāma tvam asi sa satyakāma eva jābālo bravīthā iti.*

**Перевод:**

**She said to him, “Son! I do not know your gotra. I gave birth to you when I was young, busy with service. I do not know to which gotra you belong. I am called Jabālā. You are called Satyakāma. Say to the guru that you are Satyakāma, son of Jabālā.**

**Комментарий:**

I was busy serving many guests who came to the house of my husband. Serving gurus and others, I gave birth to you in my youth, not knowing your gotra because of that preoccupation. Thus I do not know your gotra. Therefore say to the guru, “I am Satyakāma, the son of Jabālā. I do not know my gotra.”

### 4.4.3

*saha hāridradrumataṁ gautamam etyovāca brahma-caryaṁ bhagavati vatsyāmy upeyāṁ bhagavantam iti.*

**Перевод:**

**He went to the son of Haridrumat in the Gautama gotra and said, “I shall reside as a brahmacārī with your honored self. May I approach you!”**

**Комментарий:**

May I approach you as a student (upeyām).

### 4.4.4

*taṁ+ hovāca kiṁ-gotro nu somyāsīti sa hovāca nāham etad veda bho yad-gotro 'ham asmy apṛcchaṁ mātaraṁ+ sā mā pryabravīd bahv ahaṁ carantī paricāriṇī yauvane tvām alabhe sāham etan na veda yad-gotras tvam asi jabālā tu nāmāham asmi satyakāmo nāma tvam asīti so 'haṁ+ satyakāmo jābālo 'smi bho iti.*

**Перевод:**

**He said to Satyakāma, “What is your gotra, dear boy? “ Satyakāma answered, “I do not know my gotra. I asked my mother. She said, ‘I bore you in my youth when I was busy with service. Thus I do not know your gotra. I am called Jabālā. You are called Satyakāma.” I am Satyakāma, son of Jabālā.”**

**Комментарий:**

Which is your gotra? The gotra in which a person is born should be ascertained. It should be determined that the person is not of śūdra lineage.

### 4.4.5

*taṁ+ hovāca natad abrāhmaṇo vivaktum arhati samidhaṁ+ somyāharopa tvā neñye na satyād agā iti tam upanīya kṛśānām abalānāṁ catuḥ-śatā gā nirākṛtyovācemāḥ somyānusaṁvrajeti tā abhiprasthāpayann uvāca nāsahareṇāvarteyeti sa ha varña-gaṇaṁ provāsa tā yadā sahasraṁ+ sampeduḥ.*

**Перевод:**

**The guru said, “A person not of the three upper varṇas would not speak in this way. Bring thee firewood. I will initiate you. You did not stray from saying the truth.” Initiating him, he separated four hundred among the thin and weak cows and said, “Gentle boy! Follow this cows.” Departing the boy said, “I will not return until these cows have become a thousand.” He lived many years in the forest until the cows became a thousand.**

**Комментарий:**

A person who was not a brāhmaṇa would not speak in a particular manner (vivaktum) such truthful words. Brāhmaṇa here means the three upper castes (who were qualified for the Vedas), not a śūdra. Since the śūdra has a crooked nature, he would not speak in this way.

Gentle boy! Bring the fire wood for the upanayana saṁskāra. You do not deviate from truth (na satyāt agāt). You are truthful. I will initiate you (upaneśye). Vedānta-śūtra 1.3.37 explains this. Abrāhamṇa means “not a śūdra.” Because he was not a śūdra he obtained initiation.

Having initiated Satyakāma, the guru separated four hundred very lean and weak cows among the lean and weak cows. He said, “Dear boy! Follow these cows.” Going to the forest he said, “I will not return unless the cows have become a thousand.” He had the cows stay in forests full of grass and water for a long time. Then the cows became a thousand. Till then he lived many years (varña-ganam) in the forest.

### 5

### 4.5.1

*atha hainam ṛñabho 'bhyavāda satyakāma3 iti bhagava iti ha pratiśuśrāva prāptāḥ somya sahasraṁ smaḥ prāpaya na ācārya-kulam.*

**Перевод:**

**The bull said to Satyakāma, “O Satyakāma!” Satyakāma replied, “Respected sir!” Gentle boy! We have become a thousand. Take us to the house of your teacher.”**

**Комментарий:**

The bull inhabited by a special devatā who was pleased that Satyakāma had protected the cows, spoke to Satyakāma. Satyakāma answered him (pratiśuśrāva). The bull said, “We have become a thousand. Your promise has been fulfilled. Take us to the house of the ācārya.”

### 4.5.2

*brahmaṇaś ca te pādaṁ bravāṇīti bravītu me bhagavān iti tasmai hovāca prācī dik-kalā pratīcī dik-kalā dakñiṇā dik-kalodīcī dik-kalaiña va somya catuñ-kalaḥ pādo brahmaṇaḥ prakāśa-vān nāma.*

**Перевод:**

**“Let me teach you one pāda of Brahman.” Satyakāma said, “Dear sir! Please teach me.” The bull said to him, “The eastern direction is a part. The western direction is a part. The southern direction is a part. The northern direction is a part. Gentle boy! This pāda of Brahman of four parts are called Prakāśa-vān--illuminating.**

**Комментарий:**

Pleased with you let me teach you one pāda of Brahman. The cow has four limbs in the form of each of its four legs, two in front and two behind. These are the four kalās. The four kalās are contemplated as the pāda of Brahman and are called Prakāśa

### 4.5.3

*sa ya etam evaṁ vidvāṁ+ś catuñ-kalaṁ pādaṁ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśa-vān ity upāste prakāśa-vān asmil+ loke bhavati prakāśa-vato ha lokāñ jayati ya etam evaṁ vidvāṁ+ś catuñ-kalaṁ pādaṁ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśa-vān ity upāste.*

**Перевод:**

**He who knows this and meditates upon the pāda of Brahman of four parts called prakāśavān becomes brilliant in this world and conquers planets having illumination.**

**Комментарий:**

The result of the meditation is explained.

### 6

### 4.6.1
